# Athena tables

Every Athena table the archived SQL and table relations read, with who reads it. Names come from a regex over the SQL (FROM / JOIN targets, CTEs excluded) and are qualified with the connection's default schema when unqualified, so treat this as a guide, not a parser-grade lineage.
